# All official heading dispositions

Checked:2026-10-03. **OPEN / ZERO-MISS GATE BLOCK.** Documentation/candidate rows only; no product implementation or release PASS.

See [method and gate](README.md), [canonical rows](evoto-deep-audit.json), [article index](OFFICIAL_SUPPORT_ARTICLE_INDEX.md), [open heading ledger](HEADING_DISPOSITION.md). NOT_CONFIRMED means unknown, never absence. H/I do not substitute for suiteMaturity. Local/cloud, billing, identity and performance fields are candidates/unknowns, not benchmark results. Repeated options under a feature are not counted as independent top-level features.

Every observed heading is retained. UI_FEATURE_MAPPED means a reviewed exact semantic label, not a claim that its full article is covered. OPEN headings and image-only controls prevent completion.
